# Requirement-to-code traceability matrix: pi platform spec v1.4, v1.5 and v1.6

Stage 1, documentation only. Written on branch `pi/stage1-traceability` (from `origin/main` 954441c4). Code and tests were read on branch `pi/ps-flow` (worktree `.cache/lanes/flow`, tip e895e76e, 107 files ahead of `origin/main`); every implementation and test reference also says whether it exists on `origin/main`. No production code, migration, push or model call was made. The only thing run was the unit test files named in this matrix (no database is touched by unit tests).

Every column for every row is in `trace-v14-v16.csv` next to this file. This page has the method, counts, supersessions, conflicts, the ranked gap list and a compact matrix.

## How to read this

- Precedence: v1.6 over v1.5 over v1.4. v1.6 is cumulative: it contains every v1.4 and v1.5 rule, plus Part 18 (new in v1.5) and Part 19 (new in v1.6), Book 7 (v1.5) and Book 8 (v1.6). The `spec` columns give the line (`L`) and PDF page (`p`) of the row's definition in each version; `-` means the row is not in that version.
- Text source: the old extraction (`docs/pi-saas/spec-text/pi-platform-spec-v1.4-2026-10-27.txt`, `-v1.5-2026-10-28.txt`, `-v1.6-2026-10-30.txt`). Defect handled: rule ids carry a private-use glyph (U+E088, bytes EE 82 88) after the leading R or G, so `ROFFER03` is `R<glyph>OFFER03`. Every character in U+E000 to U+F8FF was stripped (python -I, read as utf-8) before searching. Stripping removes characters inside a line, so line numbers are the same in the raw and the normalised file. Page numbers come from the `=== page N ===` markers (PDF pages of that version). The cleaner page-mapped extraction (spec-text-v2) was not available; re-check the line numbers against it when it lands.
- Row ids: the id as written in the spec (`ROFFER03`, `OG1`). The families G, M, L, B, R, C and SL are reused for different things in different books, so those rows carry the book: `G1@B1` is golden case G1 (Book 1), `G1@B5` is idea-filter check G1 (Book 5). Part and bullet rows use the part number (`18.3-e`), decisions are `16.11-D3`, `17.12-D2`, `B5-D5`, audit questions are `A.230`.
- Status, strict. **Implemented**: a named test asserts the behaviour (the test exists on `pi/ps-flow`, has at least one assertion, and a unit test passed in this run). **Partial**: code without a full test, prompt-only enforcement, or an opt-in flag whose behaviour covers only part of the rule. **Missing**: nothing found after a search. **Blocked**: needs an owner decision or a human step before it can be built or closed. **Unverified**: not checked in this pass (includes informational sections and ids that appear only as part of a range). **SUPERSEDED**: an earlier rule replaced by a later one; the winning rule id is in the next column.
- Test evidence: `unit PASS` = ran in this pass and passed. `integration, not run` = the test exists and asserts the behaviour but needs the disposable database, which was out of scope, so it was not executed. The 102 unit test files that hold the cited tests were run: 4129 passed, 2 xfailed, 0 failed. 21 Implemented rows rest on integration tests alone.
- Enforcement: `code gate` = linter, state guard or decision gate, enforced before send. `opt-in flag` = built but off unless the workspace turns it on (`response_rules.brain.*`). `prompt only` = no code check. Code and test names for the Book 1 to Book 3 constitution rules come from `apps/api/tests/unit/rule_index.json` (478 entries, guarded by `tests/unit/test_pi_rule_index.py`); I re-checked that each named test exists and contains an assertion. Everything else was mapped by hand from code and test names.
- Not done: no real-model run, so no row is verified against a real model; production data was not queried (A.216, A.217 and the production half of A.2 stay Unverified or Partial).

Total rows: 764 = 576 rule or test ids + 188 part, bullet, decision and audit rows. 7 rows are informational sections with no requirement; they are counted as Unverified and flagged `context`. The family list: R-rules (RADV, RANS, RASK, RBRAND, RBUILD, RCANT, RCOMPL, RCONV, RCORR, RDEC, RDISC, RDOC, RECHO, REMER, RFUP, RGEN, RHONEST, RILLU, RKICK, RLANG, RMEM, RMETA, RNOTE, ROFFER, RPAY, RPRIV, RPROM, RQUOTE, RROUTE, RSELL, RSOL, RTONE, RVAL, RVOICE), principles P1-P26, edge cases E, M (payment edge cases in Book 1, thinking moves in Book 5), A (advice asks in Book 1, audit findings in Books 6-8), X, golden cases G, red-team RT1-RT53, learning tests L1-L14 and ladder L0-L3, solution-learning SL, Book 5 readiness B1-B7 and offer rules R1-R4, failure classes C1-C12, map tests MT, dashboard tests ST, opening tests OT, turn tests TS BT HO NG, Part 18 tests OG OF IL AA EO MD, Part 19 tests BS DQ PR NU DE SM BD, audit questions A.211-A.240 and sections A.1-A.8, Part 16-19 sections and bullets, and the decision tables.

Ids named only as part of a range have no text of their own in the extraction (`RCONV02` to `RCONV04`, `RNOTE02` to `RNOTE08`); their requirement is taken from `rule_index.json` and marked so. `RLANG09` exists in all three specs (v1.6 L5495); `pi-flow-traceability.md` C4 said it was missing, which was an extraction miss.

## Severity-ranked gaps: what to implement first

Ranking: S1 = a rule from the non-negotiables or a defect seen in a live audit with no code check, or a spec/code conflict; S2 = other Missing, Partial or Blocked rows; S3 = Unverified. CSV column `severity` carries it for every row.

| # | Rows | Status | Why it ranks here and what to do |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | G11 / TS7 / A414 / A53 / 17.3-N / A.225 / A.226 | Partial | noAssumedIndustry is prompt-only (expert prompt); the invented business was the first defect in Live Audit 4 and 5 (Critical). Move `industry_noun_gate.py` onto the branch and add a gate that blocks any industry noun before the business is confirmed. |
| 2 | BT2 / 17.2-L / A.222 / A42 | Missing / Partial | A message that arrives while pi is writing is not cancel-and-regenerated; only a stale-draft check exists. Replying to messages 3 and 4 and dropping 1 is the named 'never again' case. |
| 3 | DQ1 / EO3 / 19.3 / A54 / A63 | Missing / Partial | A direct question to pi (do you know my business, will you not ask what business I do) is not forced into the first sentence for a single message; DQ1 (honest 'not yet', then ask for the link) has no code or test. |
| 4 | PR5 / 18.6-d / 19.4-d / A.237 | Missing | Categories are still the fixed area set: a node can carry a category the owner never said, which the board then draws as fact. |
| 5 | BD2 / 19.8-a / 19.8-c / A.240 / A610 | Missing / Partial | Board, map and dashboard do not read one stored list; the operator view still redraws instead of showing the image the owner received. |
| 6 | BS1 / 19.2-1 / 19.2-N | Partial (conflict) | The price question does not start the quote (approved D-1) while v1.6 says it does. Owner decision 1. |
| 7 | ROFFER07 | Missing | After a yes to the offer, pi asks only the facts the quote needs (at most two) and says why; nothing in code or tests. |
| 8 | OG3 / 18.2-T / A.227 | Partial (conflict) | A fixed first-message template still exists; the spec says delete it. |
| 9 | DE2 / 19.6 / RHONEST01 | Partial | claimMatchesState is not implemented per item: 'noted X' is checked only against a blanket list, so a false 'it is all noted' can still pass for some wordings. |
| 10 | EO2 / 18.8-b / A510 | Partial | Owning a correction in the first clause and removing the wrong fact is prompt-only; only figure corrections are code. |
| 11 | 17.7-4 / 17.7-5 / 17.7-H2 / 17.7-H3 / HO2 | Unverified / Blocked | 30-minute outage update, 2-hour human alert, no-silence monitor and the no-pickup second channel are not found; they wait on decisions 17.12-D2, D3, D4. |
| 12 | G54 / G56 / G52 / G53 / G55 | Missing / Partial | The replays of the three live-audit chats were never run against the scripted model; the integration suite was blocked. |
| 13 | RPRIV08 / RQUOTE03 / RPROM02 / RPROM03 / RASK03 / RASK05 / RMEM02 | Partial | Constitution rules enforced only for a narrow set of cases or by the prompt: pressure for detail, claims about the quote, decide-for-the-owner and forecasts, re-asking known facts beyond name, business and location, re-asking an ignored question. |
| 14 | AA1 / G12 / B1-B7 / M1-M16 / opt-in flags | Partial | Part 18 says the checks run before every reply, but `idea_link`, `fact_ask`, `list_guard`, `box_offer`, `thinking`, `map_share` and `warmup` are off by default and scripted-only; none was measured with a real model. |
| 15 | OT9 / OT10 / 16.4 / 16.11-D2 | Missing / Blocked | Public Instagram or Facebook link: no screenshot fallback; personal-profile refusal missing; the platform route is an owner decision. |
| 16 | 17.2-D / BT3 | Unverified / Partial | Duplicate inbound deliveries dropped by message id: not found in a test (the duplicate-send test covers reminders only). |
| 17 | A61 / 19.9 / BT5 / PR6 / PR3 | Partial | A voice note skipped before it was transcribed stays empty; text-versus-transcript equality and the 'at least 3 problems and 4 wants' case need a real model. |
| 18 | 18.7-n / 18.7-ph / A58 | Partial / Unverified | Map cards are not 45% of the width and 160 px high, text is asserted at 24 px not 28 px, and there is no 390 px phone-width render test. |
| 19 | ST1-ST12 / B5-D2 | Blocked / Unverified | The number-as-password login is not built and not recommended (audit F7); ST5 (passwords never logged), ST10 and ST11 have no test. |
| 20 | MT1 / G31-G51 / RT33 / RT36 / A612 | Unverified | Extraction accuracy on 200 labelled conversations, the Book 5 scenario table and four red-team cases have no named test. |

Counts of every gap row by severity: S1 34, S2 198, S3 42 (see the CSV).
